  │ 197 +         meets = peak >= required   │

  │ 210 +     )   │
  │ 211 +   │

  │ 215 + def srgb_gamma_encode(linear: Fraction) -> Tuple[Fraction, Proo… │

  │ 232 +         # Using linear approximation for exact arithmetic   │
  │ 233 +         # This is a simplification - true gamma requires non-ra… │

  │ 239 +         conclusion=f"srgb={srgb}"   │
  │ 240 +     )   │
  │ 241 +   │
  │ 242 +     return srgb, proof   │
  │ 243 +   │
  │ 244 +   │

  │ 253 +   │

  │ 301 + def color_difference_delta_e(lab1: Tuple[Fraction, Fraction, Fr… │
  │ 302 +                              lab2: Tuple[Fraction, Fraction, Fr… │
  │ 303 +     """Calculate color difference in CIE Lab space (simplified … │
  │ 304 +   │
  │ 305 +     Using Euclidean distance in Lab space (simplified from CIED… │
  │ 306 +   │
  │ 307 +     Args:   │
  │ 308 +         lab1: (L1, a1, b1) first color   │
  │ 309 +         lab2: (L2, a2, b2) second color   │
  │ 310 +   │
  │ 311 +     Returns:   │
  │ 312 +         (delta_E, proof)   │
  │ 313 +     """   │
  │ 314 +     L1, a1, b1 = lab1   │
  │ 315 +     L2, a2, b2 = lab2   │
  │ 316 +   │
  │ 317 +     dL = L1 - L2   │
  │ 318 +     da = a1 - a2   │
  │ 319 +     db = b1 - b2   │
  │ 320 +   │
  │ 321 +     # ΔE = sqrt(dL² + da² + db²)   │
  │ 322 +     # Without sqrt, we return squared distance for exact arithm… │
  │ 323 +     delta_e_sq = dL * dL + da * da + db * db   │
  │ 324 +   │
  │ 325 +     proof = ProofObject(   │
  │ 326 +         rule="ColorDifference",   │
  │ 327 +         premises=[   │
  │ 328 +             f"Lab1=({L1},{a1},{b1})",   │
  │ 329 +             f"Lab2=({L2},{a2},{b2})"   │
  │ 330 +         ],   │
  │ 331 +         conclusion=f"ΔE²={delta_e_sq}"   │
  │ 332 +     )   │
  │ 333 +   │
  │ 334 +     return delta_e_sq, proof   │

  │ 224 +             tau_action = Action("tau", ActionType.TAU)   │
  │ 225 +             # Both processes reduce after sync (simplified)   │
  │ 226 +             return process, tau_action, ProofObject(   │
  │ 227 +                 rule="ReduceParallelSync",   │
  │ 228 +                 premises=["complementary actions found"],   │
  │ 229 +                 conclusion="synchronization with tau"   │
  │ 230 +             )   │
  │ 231 +   │
  │ 232 +         return None, None, ProofObject(   │
  │ 233 +             rule="ReduceParallelBlocked",   │
  │ 234 +             premises=["neither process can reduce, no sync poss… │
  │ 235 +             conclusion="no reduction possible"   │
  │ 236 +         )   │
  │ 237 +   │
  │ 238 +     elif isinstance(process, Restriction):   │
  │ 239 +         # Restricted process reduces if inner process reduceso… │
  │ 240 +         new_p, action, _ = reduce_one_step(process.process)   │
  │ 241 +         if new_p is not None and action.name not in process.res… │
  │ 242 +             return Restriction(new_p, process.restricted), acti… │
  │ 243 +                 rule="ReduceRestriction",   │
  │ 244 +                 premises=[f"action={action} not in restricteds… │
  │ 245 +                 conclusion="restriction preserved"   │
  │ 246 +             )   │
  │ 247 +         return None, None, ProofObject(   │
  │ 248 +             rule="ReduceRestrictionBlocked",   │
  │ 249 +             premises=["inner process blocked or action restrict… │
  │ 250 +             conclusion="no reduction possible"   │
  │ 251 +         )   │
  │ 252 +   │
  │ 253 +     return None, None, ProofObject(   │
  │ 254 +         rule="ReduceUnknown",   │
  │ 255 +         premises=["unknown process type"],   │
  │ 256 +         conclusion="no reduction possible"   │
  │ 257 +     )   │
  │ 258 +   │
  │ 259 +   │
  │ 260 + def is_deadlock_free(process: Process, depth: int = 10) -> Tupl… │
  │ 261 +     """BFS to depth checking that every reachable state has at … │
  │ 262 +   │
  │ 263 +     Args:   │
  │ 264 +         process: Process to check   │
  │ 265 +         depth: Maximum search depth (bounded to avoid infinite … │
  │ 266 +   │
  │ 267 +     Returns:   │
  │ 268 +         (deadlock_free, proof)   │
  │ 269 +         deadlock_free = True if no deadlock found within depth   │
  │ 270 +     """   │
  │ 271 +     if depth <= 0:   │
  │ 272 +         return True, ProofObject(   │
  │ 273 +             rule="DeadlockCheckDepthExceeded",   │
  │ 274 +             premises=[f"depth={depth}"],   │
  │ 275 +             conclusion="deadlock-free within search bound"   │
  │ 276 +         )   │
  │ 277 +   │
  │ 278 +     # Check if current state has enabled actions   │
  │ 279 +     actions = get_initial_actions(process)   │
  │ 280 +   │
  │ 281 +     if not actions and not isinstance(process, Nil):   │
  │ 282 +         # Deadlock: no actions but not terminated   │
  │ 283 +         return False, ProofObject(   │
  │ 284 +             rule="DeadlockDetected",   │
  │ 285 +             premises=["no enabled actions", "process not Nil"],  │
  │ 286 +             conclusion="deadlock detected"   │
  │ 287 +         )   │
  │ 288 +   │
  │ 289 +     if isinstance(process, Nil):   │
  │ 290 +         # Terminated process is deadlock-free   │
  │ 291 +         return True, ProofObject(   │
  │ 292 +             rule="DeadlockNil",   │
  │ 293 +             premises=["process is Nil"],   │
  │ 294 +             conclusion="deadlock-free (terminated)"   │
  │ 295 +         )   │
  │ 296 +   │
  │ 297 +     # Explore successors   │
  │ 298 +     new_p, _, _ = reduce_one_step(process)   │
  │ 299 +     if new_p is None:   │
  │ 300 +         # No reduction possible from here   │
  │ 301 +         if actions:   │
  │ 302 +             # Has actions but can't reduce (shouldn't happen wi… │
  │ 303 +             return True, ProofObject(   │
  │ 304 +                 rule="DeadlockCheckNoSuccessor",   │
  │ 305 +                 premises=["has actions but no reduction"],   │
  │ 306 +                 conclusion="deadlock-free (cannot progress)"   │
  │ 307 +             )   │
  │ 308 +   │
  │ 309 +     # Recursively check successor   │
  │ 310 +     successor_deadlock_free, _ = is_deadlock_free(new_p, depth … │
  │ 311 +   │
  │ 312 +     proof = ProofObject(   │
  │ 313 +         rule="DeadlockCheckRecursive",   │
  │ 314 +         premises=[f"depth={depth}", f"enabled_actions={len(acti… │
  │ 315 +         conclusion=f"deadlock_free={successor_deadlock_free}"   │
  │ 316 +     )   │
  │ 317 +   │
  │ 318 +     return successor_deadlock_free, proof   │
  │ 319 +   │
  │ 320 +   │
  │ 321 + def get_trace(process: Process, max_length: int = 10) -> Tuple[… │
  │ 322 +     """Get a trace (sequence of actions) from a process.   │
  │ 323 +   │
  │ 324 +     A trace is one possible execution path.   │
  │ 325 +     """   │
  │ 326 +     trace = []   │
  │ 327 +     current = process   │
  │ 328 +   │
  │ 329 +     for _ in range(max_length):   │
  │ 330 +         new_p, action, _ = reduce_one_step(current)   │
  │ 331 +         if new_p is None or action is None:   │
  │ 332 +             break   │
  │ 333 +         trace.append(action)   │
  │ 334 +         current = new_p   │
  │ 335 +         if isinstance(current, Nil):   │
  │ 336 +             break   │
  │ 337 +   │
  │ 338 +     proof = ProofObject(   │
  │ 339 +         rule="GetTrace",   │
  │ 340 +         premises=[f"max_length={max_length}"],   │
  │ 341 +         conclusion=f"trace_length={len(trace)}"   │
  │ 342 +     )   │
  │ 343 +   │
  │ 344 +     return trace, proof   │
  │ 345 +   │
  │ 346 +   │
  │ 347 + def trace_equivalence(p: Process, q: Process, max_depth: int = … │
  │ 348 +     """Check if p and q produce the same set of traces up to ma… │
  │ 349 +   │
  │ 350 +     This is a bounded check for trace equivalence.   │
  │ 351 +     Full trace equivalence requires exploring all possible trac… │
  │ 352 +     """   │
  │ 353 +     # Get traces from both processes   │
  │ 354 +     trace_p, _ = get_trace(p, max_depth)   │
  │ 355 +     trace_q, _ = get_trace(q, max_depth)   │
  │ 356 +   │
  │ 357 +     # Convert to comparable form (action names)   │
  │ 358 +     trace_p_names = [str(a) for a in trace_p]   │
  │ 359 +     trace_q_names = [str(a) for a in trace_q]   │
  │ 360 +   │
  │ 361 +     # For bounded check, we compare first trace from each   │
  │ 362 +     # Full equivalence would require comparing all possible tra… │
  │ 363 +     equivalent = trace_p_names == trace_q_names   │
  │ 364 +   │
  │ 365 +     proof = ProofObject(   │
  │ 366 +         rule="TraceEquivalence",   │
  │ 367 +         premises=[   │
  │ 368 +             f"trace_p={trace_p_names}",   │
  │ 369 +             f"trace_q={trace_q_names}",   │
  │ 370 +             f"max_depth={max_depth}"   │
  │ 371 +         ],   │
  │ 372 +         conclusion=f"equivalent={equivalent} (bounded check)"   │
  │ 373 +     )   │
  │ 374 +   │
  │ 375 +     return equivalent, proof   │
  │ 376 +   │
  │ 377 +   │
  │ 378 + def channel_capacity_check(channels: List[str],   │
  │ 379 +                           message_counts: dict,   │
  │ 380 +                           max_pending: int) -> Tuple[bool, Proo… │
  │ 381 +     """Verify no channel exceeds max_pending messages.   │
  │ 382 +   │
  │ 383 +     Args:   │
  │ 384 +         channels: List of channel names   │
  │ 385 +         message_counts: Dict mapping channel name to pending me… │
  │ 386 +         max_pending: Maximum allowed pending messages per chann… │
  │ 387 +   │
  │ 388 +     Returns:   │
  │ 389 +         (within_capacity, proof)   │
  │ 390 +     """   │
  │ 391 +     violations = []   │
  │ 392 +     for ch in channels:   │
  │ 393 +         count = message_counts.get(ch, 0)   │
  │ 394 +         if count > max_pending:   │
  │ 395 +             violations.append(f"{ch}: {count} > {max_pending}")  │
  │ 396 +   │
  │ 397 +     within_capacity = len(violations) == 0   │
  │ 398 +   │
  │ 399 +     proof = ProofObject(   │
  │ 400 +         rule="ChannelCapacity",   │
  │ 401 +         premises=[   │
  │ 402 +             f"channels={len(channels)}",   │
  │ 403 +             f"max_pending={max_pending}",   │
  │ 404 +             f"violations={len(violations)}"   │
  │ 405 +         ],   │
  │ 406 +         conclusion=f"within_capacity={within_capacity}"   │
  │ 407 +     )   │
  │ 408 +   │
  │ 409 +     return within_capacity, proof   │

  │  69 +     1. All threads observe the same total order of operatio… │
  │  70 +     2. For each read, the value equals the most recent writ… │
  │  71 +                                                              │

  │ 118 + def check_happens_before(op_a: MemoryOperation,              │
  │ 119 +                          op_b: MemoryOperation,              │
  │ 120 +                          program_order: Dict[str, List[Memo… │
  │ 121 +     """Check if operation a happens-before operation b.      │
  │ 122 +                                                              │
  │ 123 +     Happens-before relations:                                │
  │ 124 +     1. Program order: a and b in same thread, a precedes b   │
  │ 125 +     2. Synchronization: a is release, b is acquire, same lo… │
  │ 126 +     3. Transitivity: a hb b and b hb c implies a hb c        │
  │ 127 +                                                              │
  │ 128 +     Args:                                                    │
  │ 129 +         op_a: First operation                                │
  │ 130 +         op_b: Second operation                               │

  │ 132 +                                                              │
  │ 133 +     Returns:                                                 │
  │ 134 +         (happens_before, proof)                              │
  │ 135 +     """                                                      │
  │ 136 +     # Check program order (same thread)                      │
  │ 137 +     if op_a.thread_id == op_b.thread_id:                     │
  │ 138 +         thread_ops = program_order.get(op_a.thread_id, [])   │
  │ 139 +         try:                                                 │
  │ 140 +             idx_a = thread_ops.index(op_a)                   │
  │ 141 +             idx_b = thread_ops.index(op_b)                   │
  │ 142 +             if idx_a < idx_b:                                │
  │ 143 +                 return True, ProofObject(                    │
  │ 144 +                     rule="HappensBefore",                    │
  │ 145 +                     premises=["same thread", f"idx_a={idx_a… │
  │ 146 +                     conclusion="happens_before via program … │
  │ 147 +                 )                                            │
  │ 148 +         except ValueError:                                   │
  │ 149 +             pass                                             │
  │ 150 +                                                              │
  │ 151 +     # Check synchronization (release-acquire)                │
  │ 152 +     if (op_a.op_type == OpType.RELEASE and                   │
  │ 153 +         op_b.op_type == OpType.ACQUIRE and                   │
  │ 154 +         op_a.address == op_b.address):                       │
  │ 155 +         return True, ProofObject(                            │
  │ 156 +             rule="HappensBefore",                            │
  │ 157 +             premises=["release-acquire pair", f"address={op… │
  │ 158 +             conclusion="happens_before via synchronization"  │
  │ 159 +         )                                                    │
  │ 160 +                                                              │
  │ 161 +     # Check if op_a timestamp is before op_b                 │
  │ 162 +     if op_a.timestamp < op_b.timestamp:                      │
  │ 163 +         return True, ProofObject(                            │
  │ 164 +             rule="HappensBefore",                            │
  │ 165 +             premises=[f"timestamp_a={op_a.timestamp} < time… │
  │ 166 +             conclusion="happens_before via timestamp"        │
  │ 167 +         )                                                    │
  │ 168 +                                                              │
  │ 169 +     return False, ProofObject(                               │
  │ 170 +         rule="HappensBefore",                                │
  │ 171 +         premises=["no ordering relation found"],             │
  │ 172 +         conclusion="no happens_before relation"              │
  │ 173 +     )                                                        │
  │ 174 +                                                              │
  │ 175 +                                                              │
  │ 176 + def check_data_race_free(order: MemoryOrder,                 │
  │ 177 +                          program_order: Dict[str, List[Memo… │
  │ 178 +     """Check if execution is data-race free.                 │
  │ 179 +                                                              │
  │ 180 +     A data race occurs when:                                 │
  │ 181 +     1. Two accesses to the same address from different thre… │
  │ 182 +     2. At least one is a write                               │
  │ 183 +     3. They are not ordered by happens-before                │
  │ 184 +                                                              │
  │ 185 +     Args:                                                    │
  │ 186 +         order: The memory order to check                     │
  │ 187 +         program_order: Program order for each thread         │
  │ 188 +                                                              │
  │ 189 +     Returns:                                                 │
  │ 190 +         (is_drf, proof)                                      │
  │ 191 +     """                                                      │
  │ 192 +     if not order.operations:                                 │
  │ 193 +         return True, ProofObject(                            │
  │ 194 +             rule="DataRaceFree",                             │
  │ 195 +             premises=["no operations"],                      │
  │ 196 +             conclusion="trivially data-race free"            │
  │ 197 +         )                                                    │
  │ 198 +                                                              │
  │ 199 +     # Group operations by address                            │
  │ 200 +     ops_by_address: Dict[str, List[MemoryOperation]] = {}    │
  │ 201 +     for op in order.operations:                              │
  │ 202 +         if op.address not in ops_by_address:                 │
  │ 203 +             ops_by_address[op.address] = []                  │
  │ 204 +         ops_by_address[op.address].append(op)                │
  │ 205 +                                                              │
  │ 206 +     races = []                                               │
  │ 207 +                                                              │
  │ 208 +     for address, ops in ops_by_address.items():              │
  │ 209 +         # Check all pairs of operations to this address      │
  │ 210 +         for i, op_a in enumerate(ops):                       │
  │ 211 +             for op_b in ops[i+1:]:                           │
  │ 212 +                 # Skip if same thread                        │
  │ 213 +                 if op_a.thread_id == op_b.thread_id:         │
  │ 214 +                     continue                                 │
  │ 215 +                                                              │
  │ 216 +                 # Skip if neither is a write                 │
  │ 217 +                 if op_a.op_type not in [OpType.WRITE, OpTyp… │
  │ 218 +                    op_b.op_type not in [OpType.WRITE, OpTyp… │
  │ 219 +                     continue                                 │
  │ 220 +                                                              │
  │ 221 +                 # Check if ordered by happens-before         │
  │ 222 +                 a_hb_b, _ = check_happens_before(op_a, op_b… │
  │ 223 +                 b_hb_a, _ = check_happens_before(op_b, op_a… │
  │ 224 +                                                              │
  │ 225 +                 if not a_hb_b and not b_hb_a:                │
  │ 226 +                     races.append(                            │
  │ 227 +                         f"Data race on {address}: {op_a} ||… │
  │ 228 +                     )                                        │
  │ 229 +                                                              │
  │ 230 +     is_drf = len(races) == 0                                 │
  │ 231 +                                                              │
  │ 232 +     proof = ProofObject(                                     │
  │ 233 +         rule="DataRaceFree",                                 │
  │ 234 +         premises=[                                           │
  │ 235 +             f"addresses={len(ops_by_address)}",              │
  │ 236 +             f"operations={len(order.operations)}",           │
  │ 237 +             f"races={len(races)}"                            │
  │ 238 +         ],                                                   │
  │ 239 +         conclusion=f"is_drf={is_drf}"                        │
  │ 240 +     )                                                        │
  │ 241 +                                                              │
  │ 242 +     return is_drf, proof                                     │
  │ 243 +                                                              │
  │ 244 +                                                              │
  │ 245 + @dataclass                                                   │
  │ 246 + class ReleaseAcquirePair:                                    │
  │ 247 +     """A matching release-acquire pair for synchronization.… │
  │ 248 +     release_op: MemoryOperation                              │
  │ 249 +     acquire_op: MemoryOperation                              │
  │ 250 +     address: str                                             │
  │ 251 +                                                              │
  │ 252 +                                                              │
  │ 253 + def check_release_acquire_consistency(                       │
  │ 254 +     releases: List[MemoryOperation],                         │
  │ 255 +     acquires: List[MemoryOperation]                          │
  │ 256 + ) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                               │
  │ 257 +     """Check if every acquire sees the effects of a matchin… │
  │ 258 +                                                              │
  │ 259 +     In release-acquire semantics:                            │
  │ 260 +     - Release operations make prior writes visible           │
  │ 261 +     - Acquire operations see all writes before the matching… │
  │ 262 +     - This creates a happens-before edge between release an… │
  │ 263 +                                                              │
  │ 264 +     Args:                                                    │
  │ 265 +         releases: List of release operations                 │
  │ 266 +         acquires: List of acquire operations                 │
  │ 267 +                                                              │
  │ 268 +     Returns:                                                 │
  │ 269 +         (consistent, proof)                                  │
  │ 270 +     """                                                      │
  │ 271 +     # Group by address                                       │
  │ 272 +     releases_by_addr: Dict[str, List[MemoryOperation]] = {}  │
  │ 273 +     for rel in releases:                                     │
  │ 274 +         if rel.address not in releases_by_addr:              │
  │ 275 +             releases_by_addr[rel.address] = []               │
  │ 276 +         releases_by_addr[rel.address].append(rel)            │
  │ 277 +                                                              │
  │ 278 +     acquires_by_addr: Dict[str, List[MemoryOperation]] = {}  │
  │ 279 +     for acq in acquires:                                     │
  │ 280 +         if acq.address not in acquires_by_addr:              │
  │ 281 +             acquires_by_addr[acq.address] = []               │
  │ 282 +         acquires_by_addr[acq.address].append(acq)            │
  │ 283 +                                                              │
  │ 284 +     # Check that every acquire has a matching release        │
  │ 285 +     unmatched = []                                           │
  │ 286 +     for addr, acq_list in acquires_by_addr.items():          │
  │ 287 +         if addr not in releases_by_addr:                     │
  │ 288 +             for acq in acq_list:                             │
  │ 289 +                 unmatched.append(f"Acquire at {addr}@{acq.t… │
  │ 290 +                                                              │
  │ 291 +     # Check ordering: each acquire should see the latest re… │
  │ 292 +     ordering_violations = []                                 │
  │ 293 +     for addr in set(releases_by_addr.keys()) & set(acquires… │
  │ 294 +         rels = sorted(releases_by_addr[addr], key=lambda x:… │
  │ 295 +         acqs = sorted(acquires_by_addr[addr], key=lambda x:… │
  │ 296 +                                                              │
  │ 297 +         for acq in acqs:                                     │
  │ 298 +             # Find the latest release before this acquire    │
  │ 299 +             matching_rels = [r for r in rels if r.timestamp… │
  │ 300 +             if not matching_rels:                            │
  │ 301 +                 ordering_violations.append(                  │
  │ 302 +                     f"Acquire at {addr}@{acq.timestamp} has… │
  │ 303 +                 )                                            │
  │ 304 +                                                              │
  │ 305 +     consistent = len(unmatched) == 0 and len(ordering_viola… │
  │ 306 +                                                              │
  │ 307 +     proof = ProofObject(                                     │
  │ 308 +         rule="ReleaseAcquireConsistency",                    │
  │ 309 +         premises=[                                           │
  │ 310 +             f"releases={len(releases)}",                     │
  │ 311 +             f"acquires={len(acquires)}",                     │
  │ 312 +             f"unmatched={len(unmatched)}",                   │
  │ 313 +             f"ordering_violations={len(ordering_violations)… │
  │ 314 +         ],                                                   │
  │ 315 +         conclusion=f"consistent={consistent}"                │
  │ 316 +     )                                                        │
  │ 317 +                                                              │
  │ 318 +     return consistent, proof                                 │
  │ 319 +                                                              │
  │ 320 +                                                              │
  │ 321 + def check_total_store_order(                                 │
  │ 322 +     order: MemoryOrder,                                      │
  │ 323 +     thread_ids: List[str]                                    │
  │ 324 + ) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                               │
  │ 325 +     """Check if execution respects Total Store Order (TSO).  │
  │ 326 +                                                              │
  │ 327 +     TSO (x86 memory model):                                  │
  │ 328 +     - Reads can be reordered after writes                    │
  │ 329 +     - Writes are totally ordered (store buffer flushed in o… │
  │ 330 +     - Each thread sees its own writes immediately            │
  │ 331 +                                                              │
  │ 332 +     Args:                                                    │
  │ 333 +         order: The memory order                              │
  │ 334 +         thread_ids: List of thread IDs in the system         │
  │ 335 +                                                              │
  │ 336 +     Returns:                                                 │
  │ 337 +         (is_tso, proof)                                      │
  │ 338 +     """                                                      │
  │ 339 +     # Simplified TSO check: verify no write-write reordering │
  │ 340 +     # In TSO, writes from the same thread must appear in pr… │
  │ 341 +                                                              │
  │ 342 +     violations = []                                          │
  │ 343 +                                                              │
  │ 344 +     for tid in thread_ids:                                   │
  │ 345 +         thread_writes = [                                    │
  │ 346 +             op for op in order.operations                    │
  │ 347 +             if op.thread_id == tid and op.op_type == OpType… │
  │ 348 +         ]                                                    │
  │ 349 +                                                              │
  │ 350 +         # Check writes appear in timestamp order             │
  │ 351 +         sorted_writes = sorted(thread_writes, key=lambda x:… │
  │ 352 +                                                              │
  │ 353 +         # Verify program order is respected                  │
  │ 354 +         for i, w in enumerate(thread_writes):                │
  │ 355 +             if i > 0:                                        │
  │ 356 +                 prev_w = thread_writes[i-1]                  │
  │ 357 +                 if w.timestamp < prev_w.timestamp:           │
  │ 358 +                     violations.append(                       │
  │ 359 +                         f"Thread {tid}: write reordering de… │
  │ 360 +                     )                                        │
  │ 361 +                                                              │
  │ 362 +     is_tso = len(violations) == 0                            │
  │ 363 +                                                              │
  │ 364 +     proof = ProofObject(                                     │
  │ 365 +         rule="TotalStoreOrder",                              │
  │ 366 +         premises=[                                           │
  │ 367 +             f"threads={len(thread_ids)}",                    │
  │ 368 +             f"operations={len(order.operations)}",           │
  │ 369 +             f"violations={len(violations)}"                  │
  │ 370 +         ],                                                   │
  │ 371 +         conclusion=f"is_tso={is_tso}"                        │
  │ 372 +     )                                                        │
  │ 373 +                                                              │
  │ 374 +     return is_tso, proof                                     │

  │ 198 + def check_least_authority(process_caps: List[Capability],    │
  │ 199 +                          required_caps: List[Capability]) -… │
  │ 200 +     """Check if a process holds no more capabilities than r… │
  │ 201 +                                                              │
  │ 202 +     Principle of Least Authority: a process should only hav… │
  │ 203 +     minimum capabilities needed to perform its function.     │
  │ 204 +                                                              │
  │ 205 +     Args:                                                    │
  │ 206 +         process_caps: Capabilities actually held             │
  │ 207 +         required_caps: Capabilities needed for function      │
  │ 208 +                                                              │
  │ 209 +     Returns:                                                 │
  │ 210 +         (has_least_authority, proof)                         │

  │ 212 +     """                                                      │
  │ 213 +     # Check if all required are present                      │
  │ 214 +     required_targets = {cap.target for cap in required_caps} │
  │ 215 +     process_targets = {cap.target for cap in process_caps}   │
  │ 216 +                                                              │
  │ 217 +     has_all_required = required_targets <= process_targets   │
  │ 218 +                                                              │

  │ 225 +                                                              │

  │ 231 +             f"has_all_required={has_all_required}",          │
  │ 232 +             f"extra_capabilities={extra}"                    │
  │ 233 +         ],                                                   │

  │ 235 +     )                                                        │
  │ 236 +                                                              │

  │ 268 + @dataclass                                                   │

  │ 271 +     capabilities: List[Capability] = field(default_factory=… │
  │ 272 +                                                              │
  │ 273 +     def is_valid(self) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:          │
  │ 274 +         """Check if delegation chain is valid.               │
  │ 275 +                                                              │
  │ 276 +         A valid chain:                                       │
  │ 277 +         1. Each capability must have DELEGATE permission     │
  │ 278 +         2. Target must be consistent (all for same resource) │
  │ 279 +         3. Permissions must monotonically decrease           │
  │ 280 +         """                                                  │
  │ 281 +         if not self.capabilities:                            │

  │ 283 +                 rule="DelegationChain",                      │
  │ 284 +                 premises=["empty chain"],                    │
  │ 285 +                 conclusion="valid (empty)"                   │
  │ 286 +             )                                                │
  │ 287 +                                                              │
  │ 288 +         # Check 1: All must have DELEGATE permission (excep… │
  │ 289 +         for i, cap in enumerate(self.capabilities[:-1]):     │
  │ 290 +             if not cap.has_permission(Permission.DELEGATE):  │
  │ 291 +                 return False, ProofObject(                   │
  │ 292 +                     rule="DelegationChain",                  │
  │ 293 +                     premises=[f"cap[{i}] lacks DELEGATE"],   │
  │ 294 +                     conclusion="invalid (cannot delegate)"   │
  │ 295 +                 )                                            │
  │ 296 +                                                              │
  │ 297 +         # Check 2: Consistent target                         │

  │  38 +     def __lt__(self, other: ProcessDescriptor) -> bool:      │

  │  40 +         if self.vruntime != other.vruntime:                  │

  │  42 +         # Tie-break by lexicographic pid                     │
  │  43 +         return self.pid < other.pid                          │

  │  63 +                                                              │
  │  64 + def schedule_next(state: SchedulerState) -> Tuple[Scheduler… │
  │  65 +     """Deterministically select the next process to run.     │
  │  66 +                                                              │
  │  67 +     Uses CFS-like scheduling: lowest vruntime wins.          │
  │  68 +     Ties broken by lexicographic pid for determinism.        │
  │  69 +                                                              │

  │  93 +             memory_quota=state.running.memory_quota,         │

  │ 104 +             conclusion="scheduler idle"                      │

  │ 107 +     # Sort by vruntime (lowest first), tie-break by pid      │
  │ 108 +     new_ready.sort(key=lambda p: (p.vruntime, p.pid))        │

  │ 110 +     # Pick the first process                                 │

  │ 137 +             f"ready_count={len(remaining_ready) + 1}",       │

  │   1 + """Inter-Process Communication — Typed channels with capabi… │
  │   2 +                                                              │
  │   3 + Every IPC channel is a typed, bounded, capability-gated pip… │
  │   4 + Uses process algebra synchronization semantics.              │
  │   5 + """                                                          │
  │   6 +                                                              │

  │   9 + from typing import Tuple, List, Optional, Dict               │
  │  10 + from fractions import Fraction                               │
  │  11 +                                                              │

  │  13 + from axioms.capability_security import Capability, Permissi… │
  │  14 +                                                              │
  │  15 +                                                              │
  │  16 + @dataclass                                                   │
  │  17 + class TypedChannel:                                          │
  │  18 +     """A typed, bounded IPC channel.                         │
  │  19 +                                                              │
  │  20 +     Channels are the only way processes communicate.         │
  │  21 +     Each channel has sender and receiver capabilities.       │
  │  22 +     """                                                      │
  │  23 +     channel_id: str                                          │

  │  29 +                                                              │

  │  32 +     """Complete IPC subsystem state."""                      │

  │  39 +                                                              │

  │  48 +                                                              │

  │  51 +         channel_id: Channel to send on                       │

  │  53 +         sender_cap: Sender's capability                      │
  │  54 +                                                              │

  │  57 +     """                                                      │
  │  58 +     if channel_id not in state.channels:                     │
  │  59 +         return state, ProofObject(                           │
  │  60 +             rule="Send",                                     │
  │  61 +             premises=[f"channel={channel_id}"],              │

  │  66 +                                                              │

  │  68 +     if sender_cap.target != channel_id:                      │
  │  69 +         return state, ProofObject(                           │
  │  70 +             rule="Send",                                     │
  │  71 +             premises=[                                       │
  │  72 +                 f"cap_target={sender_cap.target}",           │
  │  73 +                 f"channel={channel_id}"                      │
  │  74 +             ],                                               │
  │  75 +             conclusion="send failed: wrong capability"       │

  │  89 +                                                              │
  │  90 +     # Add message to queue                                   │
  │  91 +     new_queue = queue + [message]                            │
  │  92 +     new_queues = state.queues.copy()                         │
  │  93 +     new_queues[channel_id] = new_queue                       │
  │  94 +                                                              │

  │ 110 +     return new_state, proof                                  │
  │ 111 +                                                              │

  │ 119 +                                                              │
  │ 120 +     Args:                                                    │

  │ 129 +     if channel_id not in state.channels:                     │
